# Skills · L · part 4

818 entries, lai-thieu-pool-design to lamprey-validation-and-qa. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **lai-thieu-pool-design**: Hồ sơ thiết kế riêng cụm Lái Thiêu trên dayboi.vip, gồm mẫu Nhật An, địa điểm, liên hệ và các quyết định đã duyệt. Dùng khi làm hub hoặc trang hồ trong cụm này; dùng dayboi-local-page-design cho quy trình chung và khu vực khác. ([huymanhmmo/dayboivip](https://github.com/huymanhmmo/dayboivip/tree/HEAD/dayboi-web/docs/skills/lai-thieu-pool-design))
- **lai-transcribe**: Transcribe audio/video to timestamped captions with Gemini (100+ languages) or local Parakeet / SenseVoice models. Trigger on "transcribe", "speech to text", "转录", "语音转文字", "generate captions from audio", or when the user provides an audio/video file with no text. If the YouTube video already has captions, prefer `/lai-youtube`. ([lattifai/lattifai-skills](https://github.com/lattifai/lattifai-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/lai-transcribe) · ★32)
- **lai-translate**: Translate captions into another language (or produce bilingual captions) while preserving segment count, timing, speaker labels, AND source punctuation density (no inserted em-dashes, parentheses, or bracketed glosses unless the source had them — downstream rendering shows every character). **Primary path uses this session's LLM directly — no API key, no model config.** Trigger on "translate captions", "翻译字幕", "翻译成中文/英文", "make bilingual subtitles", or "translate this" when working with caption files. CLI `lai translate run` is the secondary path for headless / oversized runs. ([lattifai/lattifai-skills](https://github.com/lattifai/lattifai-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/lai-translate) · ★32)
- **lai-youtube**: One-command YouTube pipeline — downloads media, fetches or transcribes captions, and runs forced alignment. Trigger when the user provides a YouTube URL (youtube.com / youtu.be) and wants captions/subtitles, or says "align youtube", "下载YouTube字幕", "get captions from youtube". ([lattifai/lattifai-skills](https://github.com/lattifai/lattifai-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/lai-youtube) · ★32)
- **lai_bu_ni_ci**: &gt; 微积分 / 单子论 / 哲学的代表人物。"宇宙由单子组成——单子是终极的单纯实体。" ([111pointer111/claude-marketplace](https://github.com/111pointer111/claude-marketplace/tree/HEAD/skills/historical-persona-distiller/output/lai_bu_ni_ci) · ★4)
- **lai_wei_na_si**: 莱维纳斯 (Emmanuel Levinas, 1906-1995) — 立陶宛-法国犹太哲学家,伦理学作为第一哲学,他者面容哲学奠基人,《总体与无限》作者 ([111pointer111/claude-marketplace](https://github.com/111pointer111/claude-marketplace/tree/HEAD/skills/historical-persona-distiller/output/lai_wei_na_si) · ★4)
- **laicms_site_builder**: 什么时候用我——用户要新建或维护 LaiCMS 独立站、传产品、写文章、美化页面、看数据或开每日更新时用我；双通道执行：环境支持时走接口回放（见发文规范增补第六条），否则走登录网页操作。 ([tony-apan/maozhishi-skills](https://github.com/tony-apan/maozhishi-skills/tree/HEAD/laicms-site-builder) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **laid-off**: Use when the user was laid off, let go, made redundant, lost their job, or got a severance agreement or WARN notice. First-week plan: dated deadlines (unemployment, health coverage, severance, final pay), reference asks and official help. US, UK and other countries. Never legal advice. ([ClarvAI/job-search-hq](https://github.com/ClarvAI/job-search-hq/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/laid-off) · 2 copies)
